- **creative-exploration**: Turn a small seed into unusually diverse, original, and useful creative possibilities through interactive territory mapping, anti-anchoring, distant association, iterative exploration, and staged convergence. Use for guided ideation across products, stories, brands, campaigns, businesses, experiences, designs, or inventions; do not use when the user only wants a quick unstructured list. ([hugobernardo/skills-library](https://github.com/hugobernardo/skills-library/tree/HEAD/skills/creative-exploration))
- **creative-explore**: Pick a visual direction before generating any creative. Produces 3-6 named, distinct visual directions (e.g. premium minimal, bold maximalist, lifestyle/editorial, retro, techno-brutalist) with rationale + sample prompts, then generates one reference image per direction so the user can choose. Use when the user says "explore visual direction", "what style should this be", "give me directions/looks", or before ads/scene/shot work when no style is locked. Part of the Creative Production set. ([DKeken/codex-skills-alternative](https://github.com/DKeken/codex-skills-alternative/tree/HEAD/skills/creative-explore) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **creative-explorer**: Activates a full creative cognitive architecture, a unified exploration, validation, and strategic system built on ADHD-powered structural recognition with embedded philosophical reasoning and strategic thinking modes callable on demand. Trigger on: "I just noticed something", "these are the same thing", "what if", "X connects to Y", "I'm onto something", "explore this with me", "there's something here", "follow this thread", "speculate with me", "think with me", "go deep", "find me connections", "what are the implications", "is this actually true", or rapid multi-threaded ideation. Also trig… ([kareem-soliman-ai/creative-explorer](https://github.com/kareem-soliman-ai/creative-explorer/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **creative-eye**: Give AI agents creative judgment and design taste. Use when: (1) creating any visual content — merch, social posts, ads, product photography, brand materials, (2) evaluating creative quality before publishing, (3) building a reference library or style guide, (4) setting up creative quality gates, (5) training an agent to develop aesthetic judgment. Triggers: 'review this design,' 'is this good enough to post,' 'creative quality,' 'design feedback,' 'brand consistency,' 'visual audit,' 'creative evaluation,' 'design taste,' 'aesthetic judgment,' 'pre-publish check.' ([LeoYeAI/agent-master-skills](https://github.com/LeoYeAI/agent-master-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/creative-eye) · ★2.2k · 2 copies)
- **creative-eye**: Teaches “design taste”; includes a 7-day training curriculum + brand-guardian pattern + cron template. ([claybowl/donjon-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/claybowl/donjon-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/osint-tools/creative-eye) · ★1)
- **creative-facilitator**: Ideation partner for divergent thinking: brainstorm options, explore a problem from angles nobody has tried, break a creative block, and generate alternatives before anyone commits. Runs a timed, judgment-free block and hands the raw material back to the caller. It never ranks, scores, plans, or decides. Trigger: brainstorm, ideation, creative block, stuck on an idea, explore a problem, explore options, generate alternatives, widen an idea, divergir, lluvia de ideas. ([luisplata/unity-gamejam](https://github.com/luisplata/unity-gamejam/tree/HEAD/skills/creative-facilitator))
- **creative-factory**: AI-powered growth marketing creative engine. Generates multi-variant ad creatives from a product positioning document + channel selection, runs structured A/B experiments, monitors for creative fatigue / CAC shifts / payback drift, allocates spend via Thompson Sampling, and feeds learnings into a persistent knowledge layer that compounds every cycle. The system gets smarter with every run. ([kriabhishek/creative-factory](https://github.com/kriabhishek/creative-factory/tree/HEAD/skill) · ★2)
- **creative-fatigue**: This skill should be used when the user asks about "creative fatigue", "ad fatigue analysis", "which ads are dying", "creative lifecycle", "frequency decay", "ad replacement priorities", "creative health check", "how long will my ads last", "days remaining on creatives", "are my ads getting stale", "ad burnout", "frequency is too high", "declining CTR on my ads", "when do I need new creatives", or mentions creative decay, frequency-CTR correlation, ad lifecycle stages, creative replacement planning, or Meta ad fatigue detection. ([channel47/plugins](https://github.com/channel47/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/_archived/meta-ads/skills/creative-fatigue) · ★1)
- **creative-fatigue**: Decide whether an ad is genuinely fatiguing or just having a bad week. Reads CTR decay, frequency and CPM against the ad's own launch baseline. Use when asked whether to refresh creative, or why costs are drifting up with no other change. ([MadalaVijay/paid-ads-skills](https://github.com/MadalaVijay/paid-ads-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/creative-fatigue))
- **creative-fatigue-angle-review-paid-ads**: Reviews Meta Ads, Google Ads, and landing page creative signals to identify creative fatigue, weak concepts, tired winners, offer fatigue, and replacement angles. Use when CPA rises, CTR drops, frequency rises, or creative performance weakens. ([Supersynergy/awesome-ads](https://github.com/Supersynergy/awesome-ads/tree/HEAD/docs/vendor/mardab96/paid-ads-claude-skills/creative-fatigue-angle-review-paid-ads))
- **creative-fatigue-check**: Workflow for checking whether a client's ads are wearing out, using the ADS Beast workspace server. Use this when someone asks about creative fatigue, ad fatigue, rising frequency, a falling click rate on Meta or TikTok, weak Google ad strength, or whether it is time to refresh creatives. The workflow checks the connection with whoami, reads how fresh the data is, calls creative_fatigue_report for campaigns with high frequency and a falling click rate, confirms the trend with analytics_compare_periods, reads meta_ads_learning_phase and google_ads_ad_strength where those platforms are connecte… ([notformal/adsbeast-plugins](https://github.com/notformal/adsbeast-plugins/tree/HEAD/adsbeast/skills/creative-fatigue-check) · 4 copies)

- **creative-spark**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "brainstorm", "explore options", "get creative", "what if we...", or selects "Let's get creative" during story or cycle creation. - **creative-spark**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "brainstorm", "explore options", "get creative", "what if we...", or selects "Let's get creative" during story or cycle creation. NOT for a bare vague feature idea where generating options would mean inventing the premise first - that routes to /craft:riff (riff first, options after). - **creative-sparks**: Offer small, open-ended creative prompts within a frame the user has chosen, such as a theme, world, lens, and medium. - **creative-sparring**: Adversarial creative ideation partner for product ideas. Use whenever the user wants to brainstorm, ideate, explore a new product/feature/tool concept, stress-test an idea, asks "what could I build" in any language (e.g. "幫我發想", "腦力激盪", "ayúdame a idear"), or presents a half-formed idea and wants it developed or challenged. Also trigger when the user wants a prototype brief generated from an idea. Runs a four-phase loop — Frame, Diverge, Attack, Bridge — ending in a falsifiable prototype brief. Do NOT skip to code or visuals when this skill applies; the skill exists precisely to prevent that. ([mondaymetime/creative-sparring](https://github.com/mondaymetime/creative-sparring/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★4)
